Smooth LBFGS implementation:
```
def smooth_objective(y, beta):
    y = y - np.max(y)
    raw = np.exp(y)
    s = raw.sum()
    if s <=0: return big, zeros
    a = raw/s
    b = conv_fft(a)
    # logsumexp
    m = b.max()
    val = m + math.log(np.exp(beta*(b-m)).sum())/beta
    score = 2*n*val/(a.sum()**2) # a.sum=1
    # gradient w.r.t a: w = exp(beta*(b-m))/sum
    w = ...
    grad_a = 2*(np.convolve(a,w)[:n] + np.convolve(a,w[::-1], 'full')[m-1:n-1:-1]) # need m=2n-1? letter conflict
    # chain to y: g_y = a*(grad_a - dot(a,grad_a))
    return score, g_y
```
Check Fourier convolution formula for gradient. Let's verify with small n manually or derive via convolve. Let a len n, w len L=2n-1. b_k=Σ_{i+j=k}a_i a_j. ∂b_k/∂a_i = a_{k-i} (if valid) + a_{i-k} (if valid). Sum over k w_k:
S1_i=Σ_k w_k a_{k-i}. Let d=k-i≥0 => d 0..n-1, k=i+d ≤2n-2. S1_i=Σ_{d=0}^{n-1} w_{i+d} a_d.
S2_i=Σ_k w_k a_{i-k}. e=i-k≥0 => e=0..i, k=i-e. S2_i=Σ_{e=0}^{e_max=i? and k≥0; e≤min(i,n-1)} w_{i-e} a_e = (a*w)_i.

Thus objective function:
```
def fun(y):
  y = np.asarray(y)
  yShift = y - max(y)
  p = exp(yShift)
  p /= p.sum()
  val = 2*n*max_conv(p)/1^2
  # gradient smooth? For exact max nonsmooth. For smooth LSE:
  b = conv(p)
  m = b.max()
  # weights: exp(beta*(b-m)) / sum
  w = exp(beta*(b-m)); w/=w.sum()
  L = m + log(sum(exp(beta*(b-m))))/beta
  val = 2*n*L
  grad_a = 2*(conv(p,w)[:n] + term1)
  g_y = p*(grad_a - np.dot(p,grad_a))
  return val, g_y
```
This is smooth and scale invariant.

Let's implement a generic `_descent(a, direction_gen, stop, seed, ...)`:
- a current; score0.
- While time:
   - Generate a direction d (LP or H) based on a.
   - Line search between a and d; accept if improves.
   - If no improve from last D directions (maybe due direction same), try perturb or switch method.
   - Keep best.

Let's detail `_lp_direction` implementation.
```
def _lp_direction(a, M, n, time_limit=5.0, ub=1000.0, seed=None):
  A = _build_A(a,n)
  c = -np.ones(n)
  b_ub = np.full(2*n-1, M)
  bounds = [(0.0, ub)]*n
  res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs',
                options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': time_limit, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9})
  if res.x is None or res.status not in (0,3): return None
  g=np.asarray(res.x,float)
  # cleanup tiny negatives
  g=np.maximum(g,0)
  S=g.sum()
  if S<=1+1e-8 or S<=1e-12: return None
  return g/S, S
```
What if LP optimum exactly 1 but direction differs with same sum? Could still improve? First-order requires S>1; if S=1 but g≠a and f*g<M for active, derivative? Convex combo might not first-order but could second-order improve. We can still try if norm(a-g)>1e-5 and S>=1-1e-8. But if S=1, normalized same g, b_ad≤M; derivative active 0 if f*g=M, else negative? Actually if some constraints inactive, derivative may be <0 even if average sum=1? LP objective max sum=1 with g≠a could reduce M quadratically. So don't require S>1 strictly; require S>1-1e-6. But if S<=1+? use. We can set if S < 1 - 1e-7 fail; else proceed. But if g=a, fail. Good.
- However if LP returns time limit status 3 with x maybe infeasible? HiGHS provides best feasible? status 3 time limit likely feasible? We'll accept if finite.
- If res.status 2 infeasible? fail.

Use 1e6. Evaluator caps 1000 only for final coefficients; internal direction can be any. No issue. Use 1e6.

```
def _smooth_lbfgs(a0, n, stop, min_time=5.0, beta_factors=(2,10,50,200)):
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    y = log(a+1e-15); y-=y.mean()
    best_a = a.copy(); best_score = score(a)
    for fac in beta_factors:
       if time > stop - 2: break
       beta = fac / max(_maxconv(a), 1e-18)
       # maybe store beta and b? Beta changes; recompute in fun.
       def fun(y, beta=beta): ...
       # Use minimize with maxiter based on time: e.g. 30
       iters=0
       while time < stop - 2 and iters < 40:
           res = minimize(fun, y, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B',
                          options={'maxiter': 20, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-5, 'maxls':20})
           iters +=1
           if res.x is not None: y=res.x
           a = softmax(y); sc=score(a); if sc<best update
           if not res.success and res.status not in (1,?): maybe if no improvement break? Could continue.
       # after each beta, recompute M for next beta.
    return best_a, best_score
```

def _resample(parent, L):
    x = np.linspace(0,1,parent.size); y=np.linspace(0,1,L); v=np.interp(y,x,parent); v=np.maximum(v,0); v+=1e-12; return v/v.sum()

Now, let's refine exact score internal and normalization.
```
def _normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return None
    return a/s
```

Let's design precomputed LP slices. Since n known per context. `build_A` called often; precompute row ranges and coefficient endpoints.
```
def _make_lp_meta(n):
  meta=[]
  for k in range(2*n-1):
    L=max(0,k-n+1); R=min(n-1,k)
    start=min(k,n-1); end=max(0,k-n+1)
    meta.append((L,R,start,end))
  return meta
def _build_A(f, meta):
  A=np.zeros((len(meta), len(f)), dtype=float)
  for k,(L,R,start,end) in enumerate(meta):
     seg = f[start:end-1:-1] if end>0 else f[start::-1]
     A[k,L:R+1]=seg
  return A
```
Need ensure segment length = R-L+1. For k=0 end=0; seg=f[start::-1] length 1 (start=0). For start>0 and end=start (e.g., k=2n-2 start=n-1,end=n-1), Python `f[start:end-1:-1]` = f[383:382:-1] length1. If end>0 but end-1 == start? length1 yes. If end=0 and start maybe? k=n-1 end=0 start=383 -> f[383::-1] length384. Good.
Alternative use `np.arange(start, end-1, -1)` if end>0 else ... But slice fine.
We can maybe fill using for k. Good.

def _build_H(lam, ctx):

  H[ctx.diag] += 0.5 * lam[2*ctx.idx]
  # sym? It is symmetric because sum_idx symmetric and diag. Floating exact? lam indexing same. okay.
  return H

Direction H solve:
```
def _h_direction(a, lam, n, mu_base=1e-8):
  H = _build_H(lam, ctx)
  ones=np.ones(n)
  mu = max(1e-10, mu_base*max(1.0, np.linalg.norm(H, ord=1)))
  for _ in range(6):
    try:
      v=np.linalg.solve(H+mu*I, ones)
      break
    except LinAlgError:
      mu*=10
  else:
    v=np.linalg.lstsq(H+mu*I, ones, rcond=None)[0]
  v=np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0, posinf=0, neginf=0)
  d=np.clip(v,0,None); s=d.sum(); if s<1e-8 fail; return d/s
```
If v has large negative for many, d might be sparse. We can also try with larger mu if negative fraction >0.5? Maybe:

Line search implementation with precomputed arrays:
```
def _line_search_arrays(a,d,b0,b1,b2, stop):
  n=len(a); factor=2.0*n
  # t grid
  ts = np.linspace(0,1,201)
  ts = np.unique(np.concatenate(([0.0,1.0, ts, np.array([0.001,0.0025,0.005,0.01])]))
  t=ts
  Q = (1-t)**2[:,None]*b0 + 2*t*(1-t)[:,None]*b1 + t**2[:,None]*b2
  maxb = Q.max(axis=1)
  score = factor*maxb  # sums 1
  # if tiny negative due clip? b arrays nonnegative; Q nonnegative.
  idx=int(np.argmin(score))
  best_t=float(t[idx]); best_sc=float(score[idx])
  # refine around
  if 0<best_t<1 and time < stop-1:
     if best_t < 0.02: lo=0.0; hi=best_t*2+0.02
     elif best_t > 0.98: lo=best_t*2-1; hi=1.0
     else: lo=max(0,best_t-0.03); hi=min(1,best_t+0.03)
     for _ in range(2):
        tr=np.linspace(lo,hi,201)
        Q=...
        sc=...
        j=argmin; tr=tr[j]
        mid=tr; span=hi-lo; hi,lo=mid+0.4*span,mid-0.4*span? Actually just shrink:
        mid=tr[j]; hi=min(1,mid+0.4*(hi-lo)); lo=max(0,mid-0.4*(hi-lo))
     best_sc=...
  return best_t,best_sc
```

Now, random initializations.
```
def _random_inits(n, parent, rng, count):
  list=[]
  # parent
  # parent + lowpass noise
  # one-sided powers: for alphas [0.2,0.5,0.8], a_j=(j+alpha)^-alpha? maybe
  # geometric: exp(linspace(0,g)) for g [-2, -1, 0, 1,2]
  # cos multi-freq
  for c in range(count):
     fr = rng.choice(np.arange(1,n//2+1), size=5, replace=False)
     y = rng.normal(0,0.3,n)
     for f in fr: y += rng.uniform(0.5,2.0)*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*np.arange(n)/n + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
     list.append(softmax(y))
  # inverse sqrt: 1/sqrt(j+0.5), reverse, mix
  # periodic high/low: 0.2+0.8*abs(cos(π k j/n))
```
Need not overdo.

Let's design `_search` more carefully to avoid infinite no-improve perturbation. Pseudocode:
```
def _search(a0,n,stop,seed=0,use_lp=True,use_h=True,max_no_improve=30):
  ctx=get_ctx(n); rng=default_rng(seed+n)
  a=normalize(a0); if a is None: return a0,inf
  best_a=a.copy(); best_s=_score_array(a)
  b,m=convmax(a)
  no_improve=0; lp_fail=0; h_fail=0; iter=0; base_a=best_a.copy()
  while time < stop-2:
    if best_s < 1.0? break no
    # if current too bad relative to best, reset
    if no_improve > max_no_improve:
       # perturb current; if after 3 perturbations reset to best
       ...
    # choose direction
    dir_type = iter % 3
    d=None; dir_from=None
    if use_lp and (dir_type != 1 or no_improve > 5) and lp_fail < 8:
       tl = 0.5 if n>500 else (1.0 if n>384 else 2.0)
       # scale time_limit with stop? if remaining small, min(1, remaining/2)
       if time < stop - tl - 2:
          d,S = _lp_direction(a,m,tl,ctx)
          if d is None: lp_fail +=1
          else: dir_from='lp'
    if d is None and use_h:
       # choose temp sequence dependent on iter/no_improve
       temps = [0.1,0.03,0.01,0.003,0.001]
       temp = temps[(iter + no_improve) % len(temps)]
       if no_improve>10: temp = min(temp,0.001)
       lam=_weights(b,m,temp)
       d=_h_direction(lam,ctx)
       if d is None: h_fail +=1
    if d is not None:
       # check if d close to a
       if np.linalg.norm(d-a) > 1e-7:
          b1=_conv_ab(a,d); b2=_conv(d)
          t, sc = _line_search_witness(a,d,b,b1,b2, m, stop)
          if t > 1e-6 and sc < best_s_current? actually current score
             a = ...; score_current=sc; if sc<best_s update; no_improve=0; b,m=convmax(a); iter+=1; continue
       # no improvement along direction
       no_improve +=1
    if no_improve > 5 and iter % 5 == 0:
       # try relaxed LP
       if use_lp:
          d,S = _lp_direction(a, m*1.05, min(1,remaining), ctx)
          if d... line search; if accept update
    if no_improve > 15:
       a = _perturb(a, rng, 0.05 + 0.05*min(no_improve/20,1))
       sc=_score_array(a); b,m=convmax(a); no_improve=0
       if sc > best_s*1.1:
          a=base_a.copy(); sc=best_s; b,m=convmax(a)
    iter +=1
  # final exact best
  return best_a,best_s
```

Implementation:

def _h_direction_from_lam(lam, ctx):
  n=ctx.n; H=_build_H(lam,ctx); scale=max(1.0, np.linalg.norm(H, ord=np.inf))
  ones=np.ones(n)
  last=None
  for mu in (1e-10,1e-8,1e-6,1e-4,1e-3,1e-2,0.1,1.0):
    mu *= scale
    try:
       M=H.copy(); M.flat[::n+1]+=mu
       v=np.linalg.solve(M, ones)
       if np.all(np.isfinite(v)) and np.max(np.abs(v)) < 1e12:
          last=v; break
    except: pass
  if last is None:
    Mu=H + scale*I; v=lstsq
  d=clip(v,0); s=d.sum(); if s<1e-8: # if v all negative, maybe use -v? Direction should maximize? Maybe use sign? If v all negative, H solution negative, clipping zero. Could try d=clip(-v). Since stationarity could be with -1? H nonsingular but sign depends. If H has negative eig, solving Hv=1 might give negative. The minimizer of x^T H x with constraint sum=1 should follow gradient 2Hx=λ1; if H indefinite, not. For direction, if v negative, clip -v? The vector solving H(-v)=-1; -v maybe KKT with sign. Try both v and -v, choose the one with larger sum after clipping.
```

Implementation:
```
def _lambda_weights(b,m,rel):
  L=len(b); tol=rel*m
  x=(b-m)/tol; x=np.clip(x,-50,0)
  w=np.exp(x); s=w.sum(); if s<... uniform top
  lam=w/s
  active=(lam>1e-5).sum()
  if active < 32:
     top=np.argsort(-b)[:32]
     topw=np.zeros(L); topw[top]=1/len(top)
     lam=0.5*lam+0.5*topw
  return lam
```

`_normalize(a)`:
```
def _as_normalized(a, n=None):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 1 and arr[0]==0 and n: arr=np.ones(n)/n
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = arr.sum()
    if s < 1e-12:
       if n is None: n = arr.size if arr.size>0 else 384
       arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64); s=n
    return arr/s
```
But if arr is zeros length n, fill ones n. Good.

`_score_array`:
```
def _score_array(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01: return inf
    b=np.convolve(a,a,mode='full')
    m=float(b.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m<0: return inf
    return float(2.0*n*m/(s*s))
```
For speed, in many calls a sum=1; but okay.

LP direction function:
```
def _lp_direction(a, m, ctx, time_limit=2.0):
    if linprog is None: return None, 1.0
    A=_build_A(a,ctx); c=-np.ones(n); b_ub=np.full(A.shape[0], m)
    try:
      res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, method='highs',
                  options={'presolve':True, 'time_limit':float(time_limit)})
    except TypeError:
      res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n)
    except Exception:
      return None, 1.0
    if res.x is None or res.status not in (0,3):
       return None, 1.0
    g=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    g=np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
    s=float(g.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12: return None, 1.0
    d=g/s
    return d, s
```
If status 3 but x infeasible? okay.

Potential issue: `res.status not in (0,3)` but HiGHS status 0 optimal, 3 time limit? yes.

def _line_search_witness(a,d,b0,b1,b2,m0,stop):
    n=a.size; factor=2.0*n
    score0=factor*m0
    ts_core=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,121)
    t=np.tuple? use np.concatenate
    extra=np.array([0.0, 0.001,0.002,0.003,0.005,0.008,0.01,0.02,0.03,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.9,1.0])
    t=np.unique(np.concatenate((ts_core, extra)))
    best_t=0.0; best_sc=score0
    def evals(tt):
       u=1-tt
       Q=u*u[:,None]*b0 + 2*u*tt[:,None]*b1 + tt*tt[:,None]*b2
       return factor*Q.max(axis=1)
    sc=evals(t); idx=int(np.argmin(sc)); best_t=float(t[idx]); best_sc=float(sc[idx])
    # if best_t==0 and score0 maybe due exact float, check tiny
    # refine if best_sc < score0 - 1e-14
    if best_sc < score0 - 1e-15:
       if best_t < 0.02: lo,hi=0.0,best_t*2+0.02
       elif best_t > 0.98: lo,hi=best_t*2-1.0,1.0
       else: lo=max(0.0,best_t-0.02); hi=min(1.0,best_t+0.02)
       for _ in range(3):
          if time.monotonic() > stop - 1: break
          tr=np.linspace(lo,hi,81)
          scr=evals(tr); j=int(np.argmin(scr)); mid=float(tr[j])
          span=hi-lo
          if mid==0...
          lo=max(0.0, mid-0.4*span); hi=min(1.0, mid+0.4*span)
          best_t=mid; best_sc=float(scr[j]);
          if hi-lo < 1e-5: break
    return best_t,best_sc
```

def _perturb(a,rng,strength=0.1):
    n=a.size
    noise = rng.normal(0,1,n)
    # low-pass: three moving averages
    k=5
    noise = np.convolve(noise, np.ones(k)/k, mode='same')
    noise = np.convolve(noise, np.ones(k)/k, mode='same')
    a2 = a + strength * float(np.mean(a)) * 5 * noise? Wait mean ~1/n. If multiply by mean, noise amplitude tiny. We want relative perturbation: strength * a + white? Better:
    a2 = a * (1 + strength * noise) + 1e-12*strength*np.abs(noise)
    a2=np.clip(a2,0,None); s=a2.sum(); if s>0: return a2/s
    return a
```

```

```
def _smooth_lbfgs(a0, n, stop, seed=0):
    if minimize is None: return _as_normalized(a0,n), _score_array(a0)
    ctx = _get_ctx(n)
    a = _as_normalized(a0,n)
    sc0=_score_array(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=sc0
    y=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); y-=y.mean()
    factors=(1.0,5.0,20.0,100.0,400.0)
    n_iter_calls=0
    for fac in factors:
        if time.monotonic() > stop - 5: break
        b,m=_convmax(a)
        if m <= 0: break
        beta=fac/(m+1e-300)
        calls=0
        while time.monotonic() < stop - 3 and calls < 3:
            calls +=1
            def fun(x, beta=beta):
                yv=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
                yv=np.where(np.isfinite(yv), yv, 0.0)
                ymax=yv.max()
                p=np.exp(yv-ymax)
                s=p.sum()
                if s<=1e-300: return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
                p/=s
                b=np.convolve(p,p,mode='full')
                mb=b.max()
                if not math.isfinite(mb) or mb<0: return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
                u=beta*(b-mb)
                u=np.clip(u,-700.0,0.0)
                w=np.exp(u); ws=w.sum()
                if ws<=0: w=np.zeros_like(b); w[int(np.argmax(b))]=1.0
                else: w/=ws
                val=2.0*n*(mb + math.log(max(ws,1e-300))/beta)
                term2=np.convolve(p,w,mode='full')[:n]
                Llen=2*n-1
                ctemp=np.convolve(p,w[::-1],mode='full')
                term1=ctemp[Llen-1:Llen-n-1:-1]
                grad_p=4.0*n*(term2+term1)
                gp=float(np.dot(p,grad_p))
                gy=p*(grad_p-gp)
                gy=np.where(np.isfinite(gy), gy, 0.0)
                if not math.isfinite(val): return 1e100, gy
                return float(val), gy
            maxiter = 25 if n>450 else 40
            try:
                res=minimize(fun, y, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B',
                             options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-6, 'maxls':20})
            except Exception:
                break
            if hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
                y=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
            a=_softmax_from_y(y)
            sc=_score_array(a)
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
            # stop this beta if L-BFGS converged
            if res.success:
                break
        # maybe after each factor, blend with best to avoid drift? no
    # final exact
    return best_a,best_sc
```
`_softmax_from_y`:
```
def _softmax_from_y(y):
   y=np.asarray(y,float); y=np.where(finite,y,0); y=y-y.max(); p=np.exp(y); s=p.sum(); return p/s if s>0 else ones(n)/n
```
But `n` needed; use len y.

In smooth fun, `math.log(max(ws,1e-300))`; ws≥1 due max u=0, but if underflow? okay.

We clip to 0. If b-mb ~1e-16 and beta 1e15 ->0.1 fine. If b and mb large? b sum1 so ≤1. beta 0? no. Good.

Could `term1` slice be empty if n=1? Problem nonempty; n likely >1. For n=1, Llen=1, slice Llen-1:Llen-n-1:-1 =0:-1:-1 -> empty? Need handle n=1. We can guard if n==1: score=2, a=[1]. But return longer. In functions, if n==1, direct: conv length1; term1 length1? For n=1, b_k=a0^2, derivative 2a0? Our term2 conv(a,w) first 1 =a0*w0= a0^2. term1 should a0*w0. Now, `_weights` function:
```
def _active_weights(b,m,rel):
  L=b.size; tol=rel*max(m,1e-300)
  x=(b-m)/tol
  x=np.clip(x,-60.0,0.0)
  w=np.exp(x); s=w.sum()
  if s<=0: w = -b? no
  lam=w/s if s>0 else np.full(L,1.0/L)
  active_count = int((lam > 1e-5).sum())
  min_active = max(8, min(L, 24))
  if active_count < min_active:
      top = np.argsort(-b)[:min_active]
      topw=np.zeros(L); topw[top]=1.0/len(top); lam=0.5*lam+0.5*topw
  return lam
```
Use top 24. If rel very small, all but max underflow to 0, active 1; blend top. Good.

`_h_direction`:
```
def _h_direction(lam, ctx):
 n=ctx.n
 ctx.ensure_h()
 H=0.5*lam[ctx.sum_idx]
 H[ctx.diag] += 0.5*lam[2*ctx.idx]
 H = 0.5*(H+H.T) # ensure sym (should but due idx)
 scale = max(1.0, float(np.linalg.norm(H, ord=np.inf)))
 ones=np.ones(n)
 vbest=None
 for mu in (1e-10,1e-8,1e-6,1e-4,1e-3,1e-2,0.1,1.0):
   muv=mu*scale
   try:
      Mat=H.copy(); Mat.flat[::n+1] += muv
      v=np.linalg.solve(Mat, ones)
      if np.all(np.isfinite(v)) and np.max(np.abs(v)) < 1e12:
         vbest=v; break
   except Exception:
      pass
 if vbest is None:
   Mat=H + scale*I
   try: v=np.linalg.lstsq(Mat,ones,rcond=None)[0]
   except: return None
 vbest=np.where(finite,vbest,0)
 cands=[]
 for vv in (vbest, -vbest):
    d=np.clip(vv,0,None); s=d.sum()
    if s>1e-8: cands.append((s, d/s))
 if not cands:
    return None
 # choose candidate with larger sum? or smaller distance to uniform? Use larger sum.
 cands.sort(reverse=True, key=lambda x: x[0])
 return cands[0][1]
```
But building H and copies for each mu (up to 8) each solve O(n^3). If fail many, expensive. We can reduce mts to 4. Start 1e-8,1e-5,1e-2,0.5. If matrix singular due exact rank, first may fail; second maybe. Use:
```
mus=(1e-8,1e-6,1e-4,1e-2,0.1)
```
Good.
Could optimize by not copying H each time? Fine.
If H+muI but H indefinite, solve succeeds often with large v; we check max. If max huge, increase mu.

Maybe H direction with v from large mu is uniform; line search to uniform might reduce metric? Sometimes. okay.

Now, `_search` needs `ctx` for LP and H. It should recompute b,m after perturb/accept.
Let's write robust:
```
def _search(a0,n,stop,seed=0,use_lp=True,use_h=True,max_no_improve=40):
    ctx=_get_ctx(n); rng=np.random.default_rng(seed*1000+ n)
    a=_as_normalized(a0,n)
    b,m=_convmax(a)
    score=_score_array(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score
    no_improve=0; lp_fail=0; iter=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop - 2:
       # if current bp much worse than best, reset occasionally
       if no_improve > 25 and no_improve % 5 == 0:
           a=best_a.copy(); b,m=_convmax(a); score=best_sc; no_improve=10
       # choose direction
       d=None; source=None
       use_next_lp = use_lp and (iter % 3 != 1) and lp_fail < 10
       if use_next_lp:
           rem = stop - time.monotonic()
           tl = min(2.5, max(0.25, rem*0.05))
           if rem > tl + 3:
               d,S = _lp_direction(a,m,ctx,tl)
               if d is None: lp_fail +=1
       if d is None and use_h:
           # choose temp based on iteration and no_improve
           temps=(0.1,0.04,0.015,0.006,0.002,0.0008)
           temp = temps[(iter + min(no_improve,10)) % len(temps)]
           if no_improve > 15: temp = min(temp, 0.001)
           lam=_active_weights(b,m,temp)
           d=_h_direction(lam,ctx)
       if d is not None:
           d=_as_normalized(d,n) # ensure
           if np.linalg.norm(d-a) > 1e-7:
               b1=_conv_ab(a,d); b2=_conv(d)
               b1=np.maximum(b1,0); b2=np.maximum(b2,0); b=np.maximum(b,0)
               t,sc=_line_search_witness(a,d,b,b1,b2,m,stop)
               if t > 1e-6 and sc < score - 1e-13:
                   a = (1-t)*a + t*d
                   ssum=a.sum()
                   if ssum>0: a/=ssum
                   score=sc
                   b,m=_convmax(a)
                   if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                       best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
                   no_improve=0; lp_fail=0
                   iter +=1
                   continue
       # no improvement
       no_improve += 1
       # occasional relaxed LP if stuck
       if use_lp and no_improve in (6,15,30):
           rem=stop-time.monotonic()
           tl=min(1.0, max(0.2, rem*0.02))
           if rem > tl+3:
               d,S=_lp_direction(a,m*1.05,ctx,tl)
               if d is not None:
                   d=_as_normalized(d,n)
                   if norm>...:
                      b1=...; b2=...
                      t,sc=...
                      if t>1e-6 and sc < score - 1e-13: accept...
       if no_improve >= max_no_improve:
           # perturb
          strength = 0.08 + 0.12*min(1.0, no_improve/max_no_improve)
           a=_perturb(a,rng,strength)
           ssum=a.sum(); ...
           score=_score_array(a); b,m=_convmax(a)
           no_improve=0
           if score > best_sc*1.25:
               a=best_a.copy(); score=best_sc; b,m=_convmax(a)
       iter +=1
    # final if best internal maybe exact? return internal
    return best_a,best_sc
```

Need `_resample(a,L)` if a length maybe n_primary. If a already length L? use.
```
def _resample(a,L):
 arr=np.asarray(a,float)
 if arr.size == L: v=arr.copy()
 else: x=np.linspace(0,1,arr.size); y=np.linspace(0,1,L); v=np.interp(y,x,arr)
 v=np.maximum(v,0); v+=1e-12; return v/v.sum()
```

`_get_parent`:
```
def _get_parent():
   try:
      h=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
      p=np.asarray(h,float).ravel()
   except Exception:
      p=np.ones(384)
   p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0); p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
   if p.size==0: p=np.ones(384)
   if p.sum()<=0: p=np.ones(384)
   return p
```

```
def _search(a0,n,stop,seed=0,use_lp=True,use_h=True,max_no_improve=35):
    ctx=_get_ctx(n)
    rng=np.random.default_rng((seed*1000003 + n) % (2**63))
    a=_as_normalized(a0,n)
    b,m=_convmax(a)
    score=2.0*n*m
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score
    no_improve=0; lp_fail=0; iter=0
    last_best_iter=0
    htemps=(0.1,0.04,0.015,0.006,0.002,0.0007)
    while time.monotonic() < stop - 2.0:
       if no_improve > 30 and no_improve % 5 == 0:
          # reset to best but keep some exploration? maybe
          a=best_a.copy(); b,m=_convmax(a); score=best_sc; no_improve=15
       d=None
       # LP primary
       if use_lp and (iter % 3 != 1) and lp_fail < 8:
          rem=stop - time.monotonic()
          tl=min(2.0, max(0.2, rem*0.04))
          if rem > tl + 2.0:
             d,S=_lp_direction(a,m,ctx,tl)
             if d is None: lp_fail +=1
             else: lp_fail=max(0,lp_fail-1)
       if d is None and use_h:
          idxh=(iter + min(no_improve,10)) % len(htemps)
          temp=htemps[idxh]
          if no_improve > 12: temp=min(temp,0.0015)
          lam=_active_weights(b,m,temp)
          d=_h_direction(lam,ctx)
       accepted=False
       if d is not None:
          d=_as_normalized(d,n)
          if np.linalg.norm(d-a, ord=1) > 1e-7:
             b1=_conv_ab(a,d); b2=_conv(d)
             # ensure arrays
             b1=np.maximum(b1,0.0); b2=np.maximum(b2,0.0)
             b0=b.copy(); if b0.size: np.maximum(b0,0,out=b0) # copy? b may be reused; use b0=(b>=0)*b
             t,sc=_line_search_witness(a,d,b0,b1,b2,m,stop)
             if t > 1e-6 and sc < score - 1e-13:
                a=(1.0-t)*a + t*d
                ssum=float(a.sum())
                if ssum <= 1e-300: break
                a=a/ssum
                b,m=_convmax(a)
                score=2.0*n*m
                if score < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score; last_best_iter=iter
                no_improve=0
                accepted=True
       if not accepted:
          no_improve +=1
          # relaxed LP occasionally
          if use_lp and no_improve in (5,12,25):
             rem=stop-time.monotonic(); tl=min(0.8, max(0.2, rem*0.02))
             if rem > tl+2:
                d2,S2=_lp_direction(a, m*1.05, ctx, tl)
                if d2 is not None:
                   d2=_as_normalized(d2,n)
                   if np.linalg.norm(d2-a, ord=1)>1e-7:
                      b1=_conv_ab(a,d2); b2=_conv(d2); b1=np.maximum(b1,0); b2=np.maximum(b2,0)
                      t,sc=_line_search_witness(a,d2,b,b1,b2,m,stop)
                      if t>1e-6 and sc<score-1e-13:
                         accept...
                         accepted=True
          if not accepted and no_improve >= max_no_improve:
              strength=0.08 + 0.16*min(1.0, no_improve/(2.0*max_no_improve))
              a=_perturb(a,rng,strength)
              b,m=_convmax(a)
              score=2.0*n*m
              no_improve=0
              if score > best_sc + 0.25: # too high (score absolute; 0.25 huge) maybe reset
                  a=best_a.copy(); b,m=_convmax(a); score=best_sc
       iter +=1
    # final exact? internal
    return best_a,best_sc
```

```
def _perturb(a,rng,strength=0.1):
   n=a.size
   noise = rng.normal(size=n)
   # smooth with gaussian approx by convolve ones
   k=max(3, n//64)
   kern=np.ones(k); kern/=kern.sum()
   noise=np.convolve(noise,kern,'same')
   noise=np.convolve(noise,kern,'same')
   scale=10.0*max(float(a.mean()),1e-12)
   a2=a + strength*scale*noise
   # occasionally random small uniform blend
   if rng.random() < 0.25:
       a2 = 0.85*a2 + 0.15*(_as_normalized(np.ones(n),n))
   a2=np.clip(a2,0,None)
   s=a2.sum()
   if s>1e-12: return a2/s
   return a
```

```
def _resample(parent,n): ...
def _generate_inits(n,parent,rng):
  base=_resample(parent,n)
  idx=np.arange(n,float)
  inits=[]
  # base noise
  for sig in (0.25,0.6):
     noise=_smooth_random(n,rng)
     inits.append(base*(1+sig*noise))
  # inverse sqrt
  v=1/np.sqrt(idx+0.5)
  inits.append(v); inits.append(v[::-1]); inits.append(v+v[::-1])
  # geometric
  denom=n-1
  for g in [0.5,1.0,1.5,-0.5,-1.0,-1.5]:
      v=np.exp(g*idx/denom); inits.append(v); inits.append(v[::-1])
  # cos multi
  for _ in range(8):
      y=np.zeros(n)
      y += rng.normal(0,0.2)
      for _ in range(4):
          f=int(rng.integers(1,max(2,n//4)))
          amp=rng.uniform(0.2,1.2)
          ph=rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi)
          y += amp*np.cos(2*math.pi*f*idx/n+ph)
      inits.append(np.exp(y))
  # comb
  for p in (2,3,5,8,13):
      v=0.1+0.9*np.abs(np.cos(math.pi*idx/p)); inits.append(v)
  # arcsine-ish two-sided maybe
  v=1/np.sqrt((idx+0.5)*(n-idx+0.5)); inits.append(v)
  # random lowpass
  for _ in range(4): v=_smooth_random(n,rng); inits.append(np.abs(v)+1e-3)
  # normalize, filter finite, maybe dedup not.
  out=[]
  for v in inits:
    try:
       aa=_as_normalized(v,n)
       out.append(aa)
    except: pass
  return out
```
`_smooth_random` returns zero-mean smooth noise.
```
def _smooth_random(n,rng):
    y=rng.normal(size=n)
    k=max(3, n//64)
    kern=np.ones(k)/k
    y=np.convolve(y,kern,'same')
    y=np.convolve(y,kern,'same')
    y -= y.mean()
    return y
```

def _hra_from_conv(a,w):
   n=a.size; L=2*n-1

```python
import numpy as np
import math
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize
except Exception:
    linprog = None
    minimize = None


_CTX_CACHE = {}


class _Ctx:
    def __init__(self, n):
        self.n = int(n)
        self.idx = np.arange(self.n, dtype=np.int64)
        self.diag = (self.idx, self.idx)
        self.sum_idx = None
        self.lp_meta = []
        for k in range(2 * self.n - 1):
            L = max(0, k - self.n + 1)
            R = min(self.n - 1, k)
            start = min(k, self.n - 1)
            end = max(0, k - self.n + 1)
            self.lp_meta.append((L, R, start, end))

    def ensure_h(self):
        if self.sum_idx is None:
            self.sum_idx = self.idx[:, None] + self.idx[None, :]


def _get_ctx(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n not in _CTX_CACHE:
        _CTX_CACHE[n] = _Ctx(n)
    return _CTX_CACHE[n]


def _get_parent():
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if p.sum() <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    return p


def _as_normalized(a, n=None):
    n0 = a.size if n is None else int(n)
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(max(1, n0), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if n is not None and arr.size != int(n):
        if arr.size == 0:
            arr = np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size)
            y = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
            arr = np.interp(y, x, arr)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        arr = np.ones(max(1, n0), dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def _score_array(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return math.inf
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return math.inf
    b = np.convolve(arr, arr, mode="full")
    m = float(b.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        return math.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _convmax(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.convolve(arr, arr, mode="full")
    m = float(b.max())
    return b, m


def _conv_ab(a, b):
    return np.convolve(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64),
                       np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64), mode="full")


def _build_A(f, ctx):
    n = ctx.n
    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    for k, (L, R, start, end) in enumerate(ctx.lp_meta):
        if end > 0:
            seg = f[start:end - 1:-1]
        else:
            seg = f[start::-1]
        A[k, L:R + 1] = seg
    return A


def _lp_direction(a, m, ctx, time_limit=2.0):
    if linprog is None or m <= 0.0:
        return None, 1.0
    n = ctx.n
    A = _build_A(a, ctx)
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    try:
        opts = {"presolve": True, "time_limit": float(max(0.1, time_limit))}
        res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method="highs", options=opts)
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds)
        except Exception:
            return None, 1.0
    if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
        return None, 1.0
    if int(getattr(res, "status", -1)) not in (0, 3):
        return None, 1.0
    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    sg = float(g.sum())
    if sg <= 1e-12:
        return None, 1.0
    return g / sg, sg


def _line_search_witness(a, d, b0, b1, b2, stop):
    n = a.size
    factor = 2.0 * float(n)
    b0 = np.maximum(np.asarray(b0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    b1 = np.maximum(np.asarray(b1, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    b2 = np.maximum(np.asarray(b2, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)

    m0 = float(b0.max())
    score0 = factor * m0

    core = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 110)
    extra = np.array([0.0, 0.001, 0.002, 0.004, 0.007, 0.012,
                      0.02, 0.035, 0.06, 0.10, 0.18,
                      0.30, 0.45, 0.62, 0.78, 0.90, 0.96, 1.0])
    t = np.unique(np.concatenate((core, extra)))

    def evals(tt):
        tt = np.asarray(tt, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        u = 1.0 - tt
        Q = (u * u)[:, None] * b0 + (2.0 * u * tt)[:, None] * b1 + (tt * tt)[:, None] * b2
        mx = Q.max(axis=1)
        return factor * mx

    sc = evals(t)
    j = int(np.argmin(sc))
    best_t = float(t[j])
    best_sc = float(sc[j])

    if best_sc < score0 - 1e-15:
        if best_t < 0.025:
            lo, hi = 0.0, min(1.0, best_t * 2.0 + 0.025)
        elif best_t > 0.975:
            lo, hi = max(0.0, best_t * 2.0 - 1.0), 1.0
        else:
            lo, hi = max(0.0, best_t - 0.025), min(1.0, best_t + 0.025)

        for _ in range(3):
            if time.monotonic() > stop - 1.0 or hi <= lo or (hi - lo) < 1e-5:
                break
            tr = np.linspace(lo, hi, 81)
            sr = evals(tr)
            ir = int(np.argmin(sr))
            mid = float(tr[ir])
            span = hi - lo
            best_t = mid
            best_sc = float(sr[ir])
            lo = max(0.0, mid - 0.45 * span)
            hi = min(1.0, mid + 0.45 * span)

    return best_t, best_sc


def _active_weights(b, m, rel):
    L = b.size
    tol = max(rel * max(float(m), 1e-300), 1e-12)
    x = (b - m) / tol
    x = np.clip(x, -60.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(x)
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 1e-300:
        lam = np.full(L, 1.0 / L)
    else:
        lam = w / ws
    min_active = max(8, min(32, L))
    active = int((lam > 1e-5).sum())
    if active < min_active:
        top_idx = np.argsort(-b)[:min_active]
        topw = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        topw[top_idx] = 1.0 / len(top_idx)
        lam = 0.5 * lam + 0.5 * topw
    return lam


def _h_direction(lam, ctx):
    n = ctx.n
    ctx.ensure_h()
    H = np.asarray(lam[ctx.sum_idx], dtype=np.float64)
    H = 0.5 * (H + H.T)
    scale = max(1.0, float(np.max(np.abs(H))))
    ones = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = None
    mus = (1e-10, 1e-8, 1e-6, 1e-4, 1e-2, 0.1, 0.5)
    diag = np.diag_indices(n)
    for mu in mus:
        muv = max(0.0, mu) * scale
        try:
            M = H.copy()
            M[diag] += muv
            vv = np.linalg.solve(M, ones)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(vv)) and float(np.max(np.abs(vv))) < 1e12:
                v = vv
                break
        except Exception:
            pass
    if v is None:
        try:
            M = H + max(1.0, scale) * np.eye(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v = np.linalg.lstsq(M, ones, rcond=None)[0]
        except Exception:
            return None
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)

    candidates = []
    for vv in (v, -v):
        d = np.clip(vv, 0.0, None)
        s = float(d.sum())
        if s > 1e-8:
            candidates.append((s, d / s))
    if not candidates:
        return None
    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
    return candidates[0][1]


def _perturb(a, rng, strength=0.10):
    n = a.size
    noise = rng.normal(size=n)
    k = max(3, n // 64)
    kern = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64)
    kern /= kern.sum()
    noise = np.convolve(noise, kern, mode="same")
    noise = np.convolve(noise, kern, mode="same")
    scale = 10.0 * max(float(a.mean()), 1e-12)
    a2 = a + strength * scale * noise
    if rng.random() < 0.25:
        a2 = 0.85 * a2 + 0.15 * _as_normalized(np.ones(n), n)
    a2 = np.clip(a2, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a2.sum())
    if s > 1e-12:
        return a2 / s
    return a.copy()


def _search(a0, n, stop, seed=0, use_lp=True, use_h=True, max_no_improve=35):
    ctx = _get_ctx(int(n))
    n = ctx.n
    rng = np.random.default_rng((int(seed) * 1000003 + n) % (2**63 - 1))
    a = _as_normalized(a0, n)
    b, m = _convmax(a)
    score = 2.0 * n * m
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score

    no_improve = 0
    lp_fail = 0
    iter_count = 0
    htemps = (0.10, 0.04, 0.015, 0.006, 0.002, 0.0007)

    while time.monotonic() < stop - 2.0:
        if no_improve > 30 and no_improve % 5 == 0:
            a = best_a.copy()
            b, m = _convmax(a)
            score = best_sc
            no_improve = 15

        d = None

        if use_lp and (iter_count % 3 != 1) and lp_fail < 8:
            rem = stop - time.monotonic()
            tl = min(2.0, max(0.2, rem * 0.04))
            if rem > tl + 2.0:
                d, S = _lp_direction(a, m, ctx, tl)
                if d is None:
                    lp_fail += 1
                else:
                    lp_fail = max(0, lp_fail - 1)

        if d is None and use_h:
            idxh = (iter_count + min(no_improve, 10)) % len(htemps)
            temp = htemps[idxh]
            if no_improve > 12:
                temp = min(temp, 0.0015)
            lam = _active_weights(b, m, temp)
            d = _h_direction(lam, ctx)

        accepted = False
        if d is not None:
            d = _as_normalized(d, n)
            if float(np.linalg.norm(d - a, ord=1)) > 1e-7:
                b1 = _conv_ab(a, d)
                b2 = _conv(d)
                b0 = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
                t, sc = _line_search_witness(a, d, b0, b1, b2, stop)
                if t > 1e-6 and sc < score - 1e-13:
                    a = (1.0 - t) * a + t * d
                    ss = float(a.sum())
                    if ss > 1e-300:
                        a = a / ss
                    b, m = _convmax(a)
                    score = 2.0 * n * m
                    if score < best_sc - 1e-15:
                        best_a = a.copy()
                        best_sc = score
                    no_improve = 0
                    accepted = True

        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1

            if use_lp and no_improve in (5, 12, 25):
                rem = stop - time.monotonic()
                tl = min(0.8, max(0.2, rem * 0.02))
                if rem > tl + 2.0:
                    d2, S2 = _lp_direction(a, m * 1.05, ctx, tl)
                    if d2 is not None:
                        d2 = _as_normalized(d2, n)
                        if float(np.linalg.norm(d2 - a, ord=1)) > 1e-7:
                            b1 = _conv_ab(a, d2)
                            b2 = _conv(d2)
                            b0 = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
                            t, sc = _line_search_witness(a, d2, b0, b1, b2, stop)
                            if t > 1e-6 and sc < score - 1e-13:
                                a = (1.0 - t) * a + t * d2
                                ss = float(a.sum())
                                if ss > 1e-300:
                                    a = a / ss
                                b, m = _convmax(a)
                                score = 2.0 * n * m
                                if score < best_sc - 1e-15:
                                    best_a = a.copy()
                                    best_sc = score
                                no_improve = 0
                                accepted = True

            if not accepted and no_improve >= max_no_improve:
                strength = 0.08 + 0.18 * min(1.0, no_improve / (2.0 * max_no_improve))
                a = _perturb(a, rng, strength)
                b, m = _convmax(a)
                score = 2.0 * n * m
                no_improve = 0
                if score > best_sc * 1.10:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    b, m = _convmax(a)
                    score = best_sc

        iter_count += 1

    return best_a, best_sc


def _softmax_from_y(y, n=None):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    if y.size == 0:
        y = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    y = y - float(y.max())
    p = np.exp(y)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        if n is None:
            n = y.size if y.size > 0 else 1
        p = np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(p.size)
    return p / s


def _log_from_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    y = np.log(a)
    return y - float(y.mean())


def _smooth_lbfgs(a0, n, stop, seed=0):
    n = int(n)
    if minimize is None:
        a = _as_normalized(a0, n)
        return a, _score_array(a)

    a = _as_normalized(a0, n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_array(a)
    y = _log_from_a(a)

    factors = (1.0, 5.0, 20.0, 100.0, 400.0)

    for fac in factors:
        if time.monotonic() > stop - 6.0:
            break
        b, m = _convmax(a)
        if not math.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
            break
        beta = float(fac) / max(m, 1e-300)
        calls = 0
        while time.monotonic() < stop - 3.0 and calls < 2:
            calls += 1

            def fun(x, beta=beta, n=n):
                yv = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                yv = np.where(np.isfinite(yv), yv, 0.0)
                yv = yv - float(yv.max())
                p = np.exp(yv)
                ps = float(p.sum())
                if ps <= 1e-300:
                    return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                p = p / ps

                bb = np.convolve(p, p, mode="full")
                mb = float(bb.max())
                if not math.isfinite(mb) or mb < 0.0:
                    return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

                u = beta * (bb - mb)
                u = np.clip(u, -700.0, 0.0)
                w = np.exp(u)
                ws = float(w.sum())
                if ws <= 1e-300:
                    w = np.zeros_like(bb)
                    w[int(np.argmax(bb))] = 1.0
                else:
                    w = w / ws

                val = 2.0 * float(n) * (mb + math.log(max(ws, 1e-300)) / beta)

                Llen = 2 * n - 1
                ctemp = np.convolve(p, w[::-1], mode="full")
                if n == 1:
                    hra = np.array([float(ctemp[0])], dtype=np.float64)
                else:
                    hra = ctemp[Llen - 1:Llen - n - 1:-1].astype(np.float64, copy=False)

                grad_p = 4.0 * float(n) * hra
                gp = float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
                gy = p * (grad_p - gp)
                gy = np.where(np.isfinite(gy), gy, 0.0)

                if not math.isfinite(val):
                    return 1e100, gy
                return float(val), gy

            maxiter = 18 if n > 650 else 32
            try:
                res = minimize(fun, y, jac=True, method="L-BFGS-B",
                               options={"maxiter": int(maxiter),
                                        "ftol": 1e-12,
                                        "gtol": 1e-6,
                                        "maxls": 20})
            except Exception:
                break

            if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                y = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)

            a = _softmax_from_y(y, n)
            sc = _score_array(a)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_sc = sc

            if getattr(res, "success", False):
                break

            if math.isfinite(best_sc) and sc > best_sc * 1.10:
                a = best_a.copy()
                y = _log_from_a(a)
                break

    return best_a, best_sc


def _smooth_random(n, rng):
    y = rng.normal(size=n)
    k = max(3, n // 64)
    kern = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64)
    kern /= kern.sum()
    y = np.convolve(y, kern, mode="same")
    y = np.convolve(y, kern, mode="same")
    y -= float(y.mean())
    return y


def _generate_diverse(n, parent, rng):
    base = _as_normalized(parent, n)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    inits = []

    for sig in (0.35, 0.80):
        nz = _smooth_random(n, rng)
        inits.append(base * (1.0 + sig * nz))

    v = 1.0 / np.sqrt(idx + 0.5)
    inits.append(v)
    inits.append(v[::-1].copy())
    inits.append(v + v[::-1])

    denom = max(1, n - 1)
    for g in (0.4, 0.9, 1.6, -0.4, -0.9, -1.6):
        v = np.exp(g * idx / denom)
        inits.append(v)
        inits.append(v[::-1].copy())

    for _ in range(8):
        y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        y += rng.normal(0.0, 0.2, n)
        for _ in range(4):
            f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 4)))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.2))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
            y += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f * idx / n + ph)
        inits.append(np.exp(y))

    for p in (2, 3, 5, 8, 13):
        inits.append(0.10 + 0.90 * np.abs(np.cos(math.pi * idx / float(p))))

    v = 1.0 / np.sqrt((idx + 0.5) * (n - idx + 0.5))
    inits.append(v)

    for _ in range(4):
        inits.append(np.abs(_smooth_random(n, rng)) + 1e-3)

    out = []
    for v in inits:
        try:
            aa = _as_normalized(v, n)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(aa)):
                out.append(aa)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(20.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + total - 8.0

    parent = _get_parent()
    n_parent = parent.size
    n_primary = int(n_parent) if 16 <= n_parent <= 1200 else 384

    try:
        best_a = _as_normalized(parent, n_primary)
        best_sc = _score_array(best_a)
    except Exception:
        best_a = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64) / 384.0
        n_primary = 384
        best_sc = _score_array(best_a)

    if not math.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_a = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64) / 384.0
        n_primary = 384
        best_sc = _score_array(best_a)

    def consider(a, sc):
        nonlocal best_a, best_sc
        if a is None:
            return
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == 0:
            return
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if float(arr.sum()) < 0.01:
            return
        s2 = _score_array(arr)
        if math.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_a = arr.copy()
            best_sc = float(s2)

    # Phase 1: primary parent search.
    stop1 = min(deadline, t0 + 0.40 * total)
    if stop1 > time.monotonic() + 5.0:
        try:
            res, sc = _search(best_a, n_primary, stop1, seed=int(seed) + 1,
                              use_lp=True, use_h=True)
            consider(res, sc)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Phase 2: a few diverse local searches.
    stop2 = min(deadline, t0 + 0.60 * total)
    if stop2 > time.monotonic() + 30.0:
        try:
            rngd = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 101)
            inits = _generate_diverse(n_primary, best_a, rngd)
            cands = []
            for init in inits:
                sc0 = _score_array(init)
                if math.isfinite(sc0):
                    cands.append((sc0, init))
            cands.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
            sel = cands[:3]
            if sel:
                remaining = max(0.0, stop2 - time.monotonic())
                each = max(12.0, remaining / max(1, len(sel)))
                for i, (sc0, init) in enumerate(sel):
                    if time.monotonic() >= stop2 - 3.0:
                        break
                    if sc0 < best_sc + 0.30:
                        st = min(stop2 - 2.0, time.monotonic() + each)
                        if st > time.monotonic() + 5.0:
                            try:
                                res, sc = _search(init, n_primary, st,
                                                  seed=int(seed) + 100 + i,
                                                  use_lp=True, use_h=True)
                                consider(res, sc)
                            except Exception:
                                pass
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Phase 3: smooth LBFGS polish on the best primary candidate.
    stop3 = min(deadline, t0 + 0.70 * total)
    if stop3 > time.monotonic() + 10.0:
        try:
            res, sc = _smooth_lbfgs(best_a, best_a.size, stop3, seed=int(seed) + 200)
            consider(res, sc)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Phase 4: optionally explore a slightly larger length from the best shape.
    stop4 = min(deadline, t0 + 0.85 * total)
    if stop4 > time.monotonic() + 60.0:
        L = 512 if best_a.size != 512 and best_a.size < 512 else None
        if L is not None and 20 <= L <= 1000:
            try:
                initL = _as_normalized(best_a, L)
                res, sc = _search(initL, L, stop4, seed=int(seed) + 300,
                                  use_lp=(L <= 512), use_h=True)
                consider(res, sc)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Phase 5: final local search on the current best shape.
    stop5 = min(deadline, t0 + total - 5.0)
    if stop5 > time.monotonic() + 5.0:
        try:
            res, sc = _search(best_a, best_a.size, stop5, seed=int(seed) + 400,
                              use_lp=True, use_h=True)
            consider(res, sc)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Final short smooth polish if time remains.
    if time.monotonic() < t0 + total - 3.0:
        try:
            res, sc = _smooth_lbfgs(best_a, best_a.size, t0 + total - 2.0,
                                    seed=int(seed) + 500)
            consider(res, sc)
        except Exception:
            pass

    out = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        out = out * (10.0 / s)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)

    lst = [float(x) for x in out]
    try:
        exact = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception:
        exact = _score_array(out)

    if not math.isfinite(exact):
        fb = [1.0]
        try:
            if math.isfinite(float(evaluate_sequence(fb))):
                return fb
        except Exception:
            pass
        return [1.0]

    return lst
```
